Extensive experiments on large-scale tool selection benchmarks demonstrate that ToolSearcher consistently outperforms a set of strong baselines in challenging settings involving iterative search and complex tool composition.

</details>


### [36] [Training-Free Pronunciation Transcription via Text-Constrained Acoustic Rescoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30924)
*Hikaru Asano,Yotaro Kubo,So Kuroki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练ST2P流水线，在推理时融合词汇与声学信息，通过候选生成与贪心搜索提升发音转写准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模TTS训练数据需要准确高效的发音转写。现有G2P和S2P各只用文本或语音，信息不完整；ST2P虽用两者但依赖昂贵的发音标注数据。

Method: 免训练的ST2P流水线：利用词典资源与G2P工具生成受文本约束的候选，再用冻结的预训练S2P模型计算整序列负对数似然，通过从左到右贪心搜索选取最优候选。

Result: 在三个日语语料上，CER由文本基线0.60–1.40%降至参考转写的0.04–0.17%，ASR转写下达0.64–1.58%；优于含训练好的ST2P模型及商业多模态LLM在内的全部基线；贪心搜索在相近CER下比束搜索快3–3.5倍，级联比直接解码快2倍；在西/法及初步英语上超过四个开源多模态LLM和最佳传统方法。

Conclusion: 免训练、推理时融合文本与声学的ST2P流水线在准确性与效率上均超越现有方法与强基线，具跨语言泛化性。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient pronunciation transcription is essential for preparing text-to-speech training data at scale. Existing approaches have different limitations: grapheme-to-pronunciation (G2P) and speech-to-pronunciation (S2P) methods each capture only partial information, using only text or only speech, while speech-and-text-to-pronunciation (ST2P) methods use both but require costly pronunciation-annotated data. To address this problem, we propose a training-free ST2P pipeline that integrates both lexical and acoustic information at inference time. Lexical resources and G2P tools generate text-constrained candidates, and a left-to-right greedy search selects the best one using whole-sequence negative log-likelihoods from frozen pretrained S2P models. On three Japanese corpora, our method reduces Character Error Rate (CER) from 0.60--1.40\% (text-only baseline) to 0.04--0.17\% with reference transcripts, and 0.64--1.58\% with ASR transcripts. It outperforms all baselines, including a trained ST2P model and commercial multimodal LLMs. Our greedy search method is 3--3.5$\times$ faster than beam search at similar CER, and the cascade is 2$\times$ faster than direct decoding ensuring the efficiency and accuracy. In Spanish, French, and preliminary English, it also surpasses four open multimodal LLMs and the best traditional methods.

</details>


### [37] [Estimating and Orthogonalizing Unknown Pre-training Gradients for Continual Fine-tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30935)
*Bing Wang,Changchun Li,Xin-Qiang Cai,Lin Yuanbo Wu,Ximing Li,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EoupCT框架，通过估计并正交化未知的预训练梯度，缓解大语言模型持续微调中的灾难性遗忘，同时保留任务能力与通用知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续微调对LLM适应真实环境至关重要，但会导致灾难性遗忘，尤其是历史任务性能下降与通用知识受损。现有正交梯度投影等方法虽能缓解任务间遗忘，却无法保留预训练LLM固有的通用知识，因为这些方法所需的原始预训练数据与梯度严格未知且高度多样。

Method: EoupCT通过带Gumbel-Softmax松弛的可学习软提示，动态生成对新任务最易遗忘的伪数据，以此估计预训练梯度；并将问题建模为多目标优化，引入一阶高效Pareto优化器联合优化LLM参数与软提示，强制新任务更新与估计的预训练梯度正交。

Result: 在多种LLM上的大量实验表明，EoupCT能有效保留任务特定能力与固有通用知识，成功缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: EoupCT为解决未知预训练梯度下的持续微调遗忘问题提供了可行框架，兼顾任务专精与通用知识保持。

Abstract: Continual fine-tuning is essential for large language models (LLMs) to dynamically adapt to real-world environments, yet it inevitably suffers from catastrophic forgetting, particularly the performance degradation of previous tasks and LLMs' general-purpose knowledge. Although existing methods, such as orthogonal gradient projection, mitigate the forgetting across various fine-tuning tasks, they fundamentally fail to preserve pre-training LLMs' inherent general-purpose knowledge because the original data and gradients of off-the-shelf pre-training LLMs required by these methods are strictly unknown and highly diverse. To bridge this critical gap, we propose EoupCT, a novel framework designed to Estimate and Orthogonalize Unknown Pre-training gradients for Continual LLM fine-Tuning. Specifically, EoupCT estimates pre-training gradients by dynamically generating pseudo data that is most susceptible to forgetting for new tasks through a learnable soft prompt equipped with Gumbel-Softmax relaxation. Furthermore, we formulate a multi-objective optimization problem and introduce a first-order efficient Pareto optimizer that jointly optimizes LLM parameters and the soft prompt, rigorously enforcing orthogonality between new task updates and the estimated pre-training gradients. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs demonstrate that EoupCT effectively preserves both task-specific proficiency and inherent general-purpose knowledge, successfully mitigating the catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [38] [FAVoR: Measuring and Mitigating Author-Style Homogenization in Federated Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30968)
*Lu Han,Jingyao Zhang,Katy Ilonka Gero,Nguyen H. Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦PEFT中的"作者风格同质化"问题，并设计FAVoR（共享-私有适配器+本地风格残差）在保持生成效用的同时提升作者风格保留度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为个性化写作助手时，多作者适配会把作者特有的写作风格信号拉向共享语域，导致个体风格被削弱。联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）虽能保持数据本地化，但标准聚合方法可能仍使不同作者的生成在风格空间上难以区分，即作者风格同质化。

Method: 在BlogText主基准上采用基于ASCE（Angular Style Classification Encoder）的风格保留诊断，并结合独立于ASCE的外部作者身份验证协议评估。针对同质化问题，提出FAVoR（Federated Authorial Voice Retention），采用共享-私有适配器设计：客户端上传共享适配器更新，作者特定的残差修正保留在本地。

Result: 常见联邦PEFT基线在保持语义效用的同时会平均掉作者特定信号，出现风格同质化。在BlogText及外部Mythos-Reddit验证上，FAVoR相较标准与个性化联邦PEFT基线提升了作者风格保留，代价是轻微的续写效用下降；消融实验、外部验证与冷启动迁移进一步支持该结论。

Conclusion: 联邦PEFT中的风格同质化是可被诊断的失效模式；通过共享-私有适配器与本地作者残差的FAVoR机制，可在数据本地化约束下更有效地保留作者个人风格，尽管存在小幅效用权衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as personalized writing assistants, but adapting a model across many authors can compromise individual writing style by pulling author-specific signals toward a shared register. Federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers a data-local setting for this multi-author adaptation problem: clients keep author text local while sharing compact adapter updates. However, we show that standard aggregation can preserve continuation utility while making different authors' generations less distinguishable in style space, a failure mode we define as author-style homogenization. We evaluate author-style retention with Angular Style Classification Encoder (ASCE)-based diagnostics on our main BlogText benchmark and ASCE-independent external authorship verification. Using this protocol, we find that common federated PEFT baselines can preserve semantic utility while averaging out author-specific signals. To address this homogenization, we instantiate FAVoR (Federated Authorial Voice Retention), an author-style residual mechanism for federated PEFT. FAVoR uses a shared-private adapter design: clients upload shared-adapter updates while retaining author-specific residual corrections locally. Across BlogText and external Mythos-Reddit validation, FAVoR improves author-style retention over standard and personalized federated PEFT baselines. These gains come with small continuation-utility trade-offs and are supported by component ablations, external verification, and cold-start transfer.

</details>


### [39] [Coupled Usage-Sense Processes: Temporal and Attributable Lexical Semantic Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30974)
*Haruka Ezoe,Ryohei Hisano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CUSP（Coupled Usage–Sense Processes），通过一个单一的边缘保持时间过程，将词汇语义变化分解为幅度、时机、机制、运动、模式与文本证据六个兼容视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常用独立采样的时期分布间的标量距离来概括词汇语义变化，只能衡量"变化了多少"，无法揭示"何时变化、由哪些机制和成分运动驱动、哪些用法支撑该归因"。

Method: CUSP构建单一边缘保持的时序过程：用层级耦合通过潜在用法成分关联上下文分布，用马尔可夫组合使相邻与长跨度的对应关系相容；引入位移算子量化变化幅度与时机，将变异精确分解为成分中心运动与成分内重组，并归因到被输运的成分对；用词局部模式刻画不同变化方向及其时间活跃度，并以被归因成分的代表性文本段落作为证据。在高斯混合特化下证明算子和平方距离的参数可恢复性。

Result: 合成实验验证了理论预测的收敛速率；CUSP在英语和德语DWUG上保持竞争力，并能恢复受控的Janus剖面，同时维持组合一致的输运；在大型美国法院意见语料上展示了无标注自然文本中的转变、模式与段落归因。

Conclusion: CUSP使变化幅度、时机、机制、运动、模式与文本证据成为同一词汇历史的兼容视角，弥补了标量距离方法的解释性不足。

Abstract: Lexical semantic change is usually summarized by a scalar distance between independently sampled period distributions. This measures how much a word changed, but does not reveal when it changed, which mechanisms and component movements carried the change, or which usages support the attribution. We introduce Coupled Usage--Sense Processes (CUSP), which derives these answers from a single marginal preserving temporal process. A hierarchical coupling relates contextual distributions through latent usage components, while Markov composition makes adjacent and longer span correspondences compatible. Displacement operators quantify change magnitude and timing, split variation exactly between movement of component centers and reorganization within components, and attribute it to transported component pairs. Word-local modes resolve distinct directions of change and their activity over time, while representative passages from attributed components ground the analysis in text. Under a Gaussian mixture specialization, we prove parametric recovery of the operators and squared distances. Synthetic experiments support the predicted rate. CUSP remains competitive on English and German DWUG and recovers controlled Janus profiles while maintaining compositionally coherent transport. A large corpus of US court opinions demonstrates transition, mode, and passage attribution in unlabeled natural text. CUSP thus makes magnitude, timing, mechanism, movement, modes, and textual evidence compatible views of one lexical history.

</details>


### [40] [THA: Weighted Finite-State Text Normalization and Inverse Text Normalization for Khmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30984)
*Seanghay Yath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Tha，一个基于加权有限状态转换器（WFST）的高棉语文本正则化（TN）与逆文本正则化（ITN）开源工具包，并通过分词与音节边界约束提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高棉语在TTS与ASR两个方向上都缺乏维护良好的开源工具，且其书写系统带来两大难点：词间不以空格分隔，以及数字词会出现在普通词内部，导致TN/ITN困难。

Method: 采用加权有限状态转换器构建Tha：第一个转换器在一次最短路搜索中完成整行的分词与分类；第二个转换器用于拒绝高棉语音节内部的错误token边界。

Result: 在Google高棉语测试集上，274个基数词全部与参考一致（仅一个拼写变体差异）；在2,906条真实TTS提示中，Tha改写的158个句子有153个正确。

Conclusion: Tha以Apache 2.0许可证开源，为高棉语TN与ITN提供了可维护且高精度的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-speech needs written text in spoken form, and speech recognition output needs the reverse. For Khmer, neither direction has a maintained open-source tool, and the script makes both harder: words are not separated by spaces, and number words occur inside ordinary words. We present Tha, a Khmer text normalization and inverse text normalization toolkit built from weighted finite-state transducers. It segments and classifies a whole line in one shortest-path search, and a second transducer rejects token boundaries inside a Khmer syllable. On Google's Khmer test suite, Tha agrees with the reference on all 274 cardinals up to one spelling variant, and on 2,906 real TTS prompts, 153 of the 158 sentences it rewrites are correct. Tha is open source under the Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [41] [Evaluating Sycophancy in Chinese Large Language Models on Factual Questions Derived from Online Search Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30986)
*Geng Liu,Feng Li,Mengxiao Zhu,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文分析了中文事实核查场景下大语言模型的"sycophancy"（迎合用户错误信念）现象，通过36万余条回答追踪正确、错误、不确定回答之间的转移，发现推理并非可靠防护，反迎合提示在减少错误认同的同时会增加不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型日益成为信息获取的中介，需保证事实准确与独立。但模型可能表现出sycophancy，即使用户信念错误也迎合，从而把错误信息伪装成独立验证的结论，强化用户对虚假主张的信心。既有研究尚未厘清：引入用户信念是使原本正确的回答变为错误或不确定，还是使不确定回答变为迎合性错误；也不清楚反迎合干预是保留/恢复事实准确性，还是仅将回答推向不确定。

Method: 采用中文是非型事实核查问题，覆盖3个中文前沿大模型（DeepSeek、Qwen、Doubao），取自真实中文搜索查询的12,165个事实问题，共364,941条回答。在基线、信念条件化、反迎合提示三种设置下评估，并有/无推理两种模式。通过配对追踪正确、错误、不确定回答之间的转移；在用户信念错误时区分"信念对齐型错误"与"事实信心丧失"（原本正确变为不确定）。

Result: 模式因模型与推理设置而异：推理并非一致的保障；反迎合指令能减少错误认同，但会增加不确定性。

Conclusion: 在中文事实问答中，避免迎合错误信念不等同于保持事实准确性，凸显了"转移层面"评估的价值。此类行为可能通过强化错误信息或削弱用户对正确回答的信心，损害大模型中介信息获取的可靠性。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly mediate information access, factually accurate and independent answers are critical. However, these models can exhibit sycophancy by aligning their responses with users' stated beliefs even when those beliefs are incorrect, potentially presenting misinformation as independently verified and reinforcing users' confidence in false claims. Prior work leaves unresolved whether introducing user beliefs causes correct responses to become incorrect or uncertain, or causes uncertain responses to become belief-aligned incorrect answers. It also remains unclear whether anti-sycophancy interventions preserve or restore factual accuracy or merely shift responses toward uncertainty. We analyze factual sycophancy in Chinese-language information seeking using yes/no fact-checking questions. Our analysis covers 364,941 responses from three frontier Chinese-based LLMs (DeepSeek, Qwen, and Doubao) to 12,165 factual questions derived from real-world Chinese search queries. We evaluate the models with and without reasoning across baseline, belief-conditioned, and anti-sycophancy prompting, tracing matched shifts among correct, incorrect, and uncertain responses. Under incorrect user beliefs, we distinguish belief-aligned errors from losses of factual confidence, in which initially correct answers become uncertain. Patterns vary across models and reasoning settings: reasoning is not a consistent safeguard, and anti-sycophancy instructions can reduce incorrect agreement while increasing uncertainty. In Chinese-language factual question answering, avoiding agreement with false beliefs is therefore not equivalent to preserving factual accuracy, highlighting the value of transition-level evaluation. Such behavior may undermine the reliability of LLM-mediated information access by reinforcing misinformation or weakening users' confidence in factually correct answers.

</details>


### [42] [ZooWork-ShopRanker: An Open, Preference-Aligned E-Commerce Reranker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31002)
*Siqiao Xue,Shuxuan Liu,Ning Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出电商重排模型家族 ZooWork-ShopRanker（0.6B/4B/8B），以多家族推理 LLM 作为偏好标注的"oracle"、结合位置去偏与一致性分级生成训练对，并用 8B 模型蒸馏到 4B/0.6B；同时发布约 1 万条私有流量偏好对的基准 ShopRank-Bench，模型在两种文本格式及 MTEB 上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用网页检索的开放重排器迁移到电商场景效果不佳，因为电商排序不仅依赖主题相关性，还取决于用户偏好、商品约束与商品间比较适配度。这类偏好信号难以大规模监督：真实搜索流量虽提供真实查询与候选，却缺乏干净的成对标签。

Method: 1) 构建 0.6B、4B、8B 的电商重排器家族，对齐"评判者标注的购物偏好"；2) 由不同家族的推理大模型组成偏好 oracle 面板标注训练对，采用位置去偏判断与一致性分级；3) 对齐后的 8B 旗舰作为蒸馏教师，指导高效的 4B 与 0.6B 模型拟合其分数并在判定对上锐化；4) 构建 ShopRank-Bench：约 1 万条私有流量偏好对、两种文本格式、按承诺该标签的评判家族数量分级，并做污染限制。

Result: ZooWork-ShopRanker-8B 与 -4B 显著优于最强开放重排基线；每个模型均显著优于其未对齐的基座；ZooWork-ShopRanker-0.6B 优于同尺寸同类模型；增益在两种文本格式下均成立，并扩展到常用 MTEB 基准。

Conclusion: 通过推理 LLM 面板标注偏好、位置去偏与一致性分级，以及旗舰到小模型的对齐蒸馏，可有效将开放重排器适配到电商偏好排序；作者发布模型与双格式 ShopRank-Bench 以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Open rerankers trained for general web retrieval transfer imperfectly to e-commerce, where ranking decisions depend not only on topical relevance but also on user preferences, product constraints, and comparative product fit. These preference signals are difficult to supervise at scale: real search traffic provides authentic queries and candidates but no clean pairwise labels. We present ZooWork-ShopRanker, a family of e-commerce rerankers (0.6B, 4B, and 8B) aligned to judge-labeled shopping preference. Training pairs are labeled by a panel of reasoning large language models (LLMs) from different families acting as a preference oracle, with position-debiased judgments and agreement tiers, and the rerankers are trained on these labels. The aligned 8B flagship then serves as a distillation teacher for the efficient 4B and 0.6B models, which are fit to its scores and sharpened on judged pairs. To measure progress, we introduce ShopRank-Bench, a contamination-limited benchmark of ~10,000 private-traffic preference pairs in both text formats, tiered by how many judge families committed to each label. ZooWork-ShopRanker-8B and -4B significantly outperform the strongest open reranker baseline, every model significantly beats its own un-aligned base, and ZooWork-ShopRanker-0.6B beats its size peer; the gains hold in both formats and extend to common MTEB benchmarks. We release the models and the dual-format ShopRank-Bench to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [43] [G$^2$PTQ: Improving LLM Post-Training Quantization with Generalized Gradient Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31009)
*Ruikang Liu,Haoli Bai,Yuxuan Sun,Qian Zhang,Wenzheng Cai,Yanqi Hao,Feiyu Wang,Weidong Zhong,Zhuang Wang,Tong Yang,Xiangsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出G$^2$PTQ框架，结合一阶与二阶信息，在全局监督的块级优化目标下进行训练后量化，缓解梯度与Hessian估计陈旧问题，并优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPTQ类PTQ方法存在两类互补缺陷：局部逐层目标缺乏全局监督；全局目标方法在开始时固定Hessian估计并忽略一阶梯度，导致量化过程中引导信息逐渐陈旧。

Method: G$^2$PTQ在全局监督的块级优化目标下整合一阶与二阶信息；在量化每个Transformer块前刷新梯度与Hessian估计以避免陈旧；引入信赖域缩放机制动态约束梯度步长防止权重更新爆炸；并推导块级Hessian近似与精确梯度补偿的高效实现。

Result: 在多种模型家族和比特宽度上的实验表明，G$^2$PTQ与全精度模型对齐更好，性能优于当前最先进基线。

Conclusion: G$^2$PTQ是一种统一且高效的PTQ框架，通过广义梯度补偿与全局监督块级优化，在不重训练的情况下提升LLM量化后与全精度模型的一致性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a practical approach to reducing the memory and computational footprint of large language models (LLMs) without retraining. GPTQ-based methods have become the de facto standard, yet they suffer from two complementary limitations. Methods with local, layer-wise objectives lack global supervision; while methods with global objectives fix their Hessian estimates at the start and ignore first-order gradients, so their guidance grows stale as quantization proceeds. This paper presents G$^2$PTQ, a unified PTQ framework with Generalized Gradient Compensation that integrates both first- and second-order information under a globally supervised, block-wise optimization objective. By refreshing gradient and Hessian estimates before quantizing each Transformer block, G$^2$PTQ avoids the staleness of prior global methods. Furthermore, to stabilize the exact first-order compensation, we introduce a trust-region scaling mechanism that dynamically bounds the gradient step to prevent exploding weight updates. Finally, we derive efficient implementations for block-wise Hessian approximation and exact gradient compensation. Experimental results on various model families and bit-widths demonstrate that G$^2$PTQ enables better alignment with the full-precision model, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/G2PTQ/G2PTQ.

</details>


### [44] [Modeling Student Sensemaking with LLMs and Knowledge-Graph-Guided Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31046)
*Özge Alacam,Zübeyde Demet Kirbulut Güneş,Funda Ekici,Nurcan Turan-Oluk,Dilay Dinçdemir,Hakkı Kadayıfçı,Sevinç Nihal Yeşiloğlu,Burcu Işık,Halil Tümay,Sinem Gencer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估中小规模指令微调LLM在无任务特定训练条件下对协作科学学习中多维度意义建构的分析能力，发现推理提示与知识状态诊断信息可提升对失败案例的识别并与专家标注更一致，但无单一配置在所有维度上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作科学学习需要对学习者对话进行细致解读，以刻画其如何识别知识缺口、建构解释并推进问题解决。这类理论驱动的分析劳动强度大、难以规模化，因此研究探索LLM能否在无需任务特定训练的情况下支持此类分析。

Method: 在23个标注丰富、经专家标注的协作片段上评估两个中等规模LLM，采用多种提示条件，变化定义性脚手架、推理模式与话轮结构；同时考察引入结构化知识状态信息对模型推理的影响。

Result: 无推理时模型倾向高估成功意义建构；启用推理的提示改善了失败案例识别。知识状态诊断提供额外依据，提升失败意义建构检测率并与专家标注一致性更高。没有单一配置在所有意义建构维度上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 指令微调LLM可在无需任务特定训练下支持协作意义建构的多维分析，推理模式与知识状态信息有助于提升分析质量，但任务本身的多维性决定了不存在普适最优配置。

Abstract: Collaborative science learning requires nuanced interpretation of student dialogue to characterize how learners identify knowledge gaps, build explanations, and work toward resolution - a theory-driven analysis that is labor-intensive and difficult to scale. We investigate whether instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) can support multidimensional analysis of collaborative sensemaking without task-specific training, and whether structured knowledge-state information improves model inference. We evaluate two mid-size LLMs on 23 richly annotated, expert-labeled episodes across prompting conditions that vary definitional scaffolding, reasoning mode, and turn structure. Without reasoning, models tend to overpredict successful sensemaking; reasoning-enabled prompting improves identification of unsuccessful cases. Knowledge-state diagnostics provide additional grounding, improving detection of unsuccessful sensemaking and increasing agreement with expert annotations. No single configuration performs best across all sensemaking dimensions, underscoring the multidimensional nature of the task.

</details>


### [45] [CG-Probes: Recovering Guardrail Directions from Patient Query Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31062)
*Marko Řeháček,Vítězslav Dušek,Martin Rusinko,Vít Nováček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出临床护栏探针（CG-Probes），通过在冻结嵌入空间中用均值差方法探测肿瘤学查询的嵌入向量，将三个有序风险轴（医疗紧迫性、心理紧迫性、话题敏感性）建模为线性方向，实现低成本的患者查询风险分级，性能与开源大模型相当但延迟大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向患者的AI助手虽有价值，但接收到的查询可能带来医学风险，需要设置护栏。因此需要一种可测量查询风险、可被临床医生检查并据以设定升级阈值的方法。

Method: 与肿瘤科医生合作定义三个有序风险轴：医疗紧迫性、心理紧迫性、话题敏感性。在冻结嵌入器的归一化嵌入空间中，用均值差（difference-in-means）方法将每个轴视为潜在线性方向进行探测。用BERTopic对79,658条捷克语肿瘤学搜索查询聚类，再通过少样本提示由聚类生成风险等级对比的查询对用于训练探针。在200条查询（90条真实、110条合成）上评估，每条由两位肿瘤科医生评分，与两个开源权重LLM及一个前沿LLM对比。仅需搜索日志、轴定义和嵌入模型的黑盒访问。

Result: 基于紧迫性的轴可作为线性方向被恢复；探针与开源权重LLM表现相当（二次加权kappa无显著差异），但延迟仅为后者的一小部分。每个轴输出一个标量分数，供临床医生检查并设定升级阈值。

Conclusion: 该流程仅依赖搜索日志、轴定义和嵌入模型黑盒访问，提示其可迁移至其他医疗领域。在新查询和新风险轴上的稳健验证仍属未来工作。

Abstract: Patient-facing AI assistants promise valuable support to patients, but incoming queries can pose medical risks. To create guardrails, we work with oncologists to define three ordinal risk axes: Medical Urgency, Psychological Urgency, and Topic Sensitivity. We propose Clinical Guardrail Probes (CG-Probes) to measure the risks from query embeddings. We probe for each axis in the normalized embedding space of frozen embedders via the difference-in-means method, treating each axis as a potential linear direction. To train the probes, we cluster 79,658 Czech oncology search queries with BERTopic and use these clusters to generate pairs of queries with contrastive risk levels via few-shot prompting. We evaluate the approach on 200 queries (90 real, 110 synthetic), each graded by two oncologists, against two open-weight LLMs and a frontier LLM. We find that urgency-based axes are recoverable as linear directions, and the probes are competitive with open-weight LLMs (no significant differences in quadratic-weighted kappa) at a fraction of the latency. Each axis yields a scalar score that clinicians can inspect and use to set escalation thresholds. The pipeline requires only search logs, axis definitions, and black-box access to the embedding model, suggesting transferability across healthcare domains. Robust validation on new queries and axes remains future work.

</details>


### [46] [LocUS: Head Selection and Subspace Projection for Targeted Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31122)
*Irene Tallini,Lorenzo Basile,Valentino Maiorca,Francesco Locatello,Alberto Cazzaniga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LocUS是一种基于输出词表子空间的激活引导方法，通过在unembedding矩阵中识别属性特定的线性子空间，将引导变换约束到特定子空间并定位到稀疏的注意力头子集上，从而在毒性缓解、情感重定向和谄媚抑制任务中匹配或超越SOTA基线，同时仅干预不到6%的参数并更好地保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准激活引导方法从对比数据中估计每层的引导方向，并将其应用于该层的整个表示空间，这可能导致干预与对比数据中的非目标属性耦合，从而损害无关能力。需要一种更精细的引导方式以减少这种副作用。

Method: LocUS（Localized Unembedding Steering）将激活引导锚定到模型自身的输出词表子空间。具体地，在unembedding矩阵中识别属性特定的线性子空间，施加几何约束将引导变换限制在该子空间内，同时将应用范围局部化到稀疏的注意力头子集上。

Result: 在三个模型家族上对毒性缓解、情感重定向和谄媚抑制进行广泛评估，LocUS匹配或超越了当前最优基线，同时仅干预不到6%的参数，并更好地保持了通用能力。

Conclusion: 将激活引导锚定到输出词表子空间并进行稀疏局部化，是一种有效且参数高效的激活引导范式，能够在实现目标属性控制的同时最小化对无关能力的损害。

Abstract: Activation steering is a powerful training-free paradigm for controlling large language models at inference time. However, standard approaches estimate a per-layer steering direction from contrastive data and apply it on the layer's entire representation space, which may couple the intervention to off-target properties present in the contrastive data and degrade unrelated capabilities. To mitigate this issue, we introduce LocUS (Localized Unembedding Steering), a method which grounds activation steering to the model's own output vocabulary subspace. By identifying a property-specific linear subspace within the unembedding matrix, LocUS enforces a geometric constraint that restricts the steering transformation to a specific subspace and at the same time localizes its application to a sparse subset of attention heads. Extensive evaluations across three model families on toxicity mitigation, sentiment redirection and sycophancy suppression show that LocUS matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while intervening on under 6% of parameters and better preserving general capability.

</details>


### [47] [Do we need to answer that question? Salience and Answerability of Potential Questions in Naturalistic Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31130)
*Amandine Decker,Maxime Amblard,Ellen Breitholtz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究以英国国家语料库对话为数据，构建7,124个自动生成问题并标注其显著性与可回答性，发现对话中问题显著性与后续被回应（可回答性）存在稳健但低度的正相关，且该效应明显弱于独白文本，说明会话结构更不可预测；结构化互动中标注者一致性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"讨论中的问题"（QUD）模型在自然对话中的适用性，即生成的潜在问题之显著性是否能预测其随后被回应，并与独白文本对比以考察会话结构的可预测性。

Method: 基于Wu et al. (2024)，从英国国家语料库的话语及前文语境中自动生成7,124个问题，并对其显著性与可回答性进行标注，进而分析二者的相关性及不同对话类型下标注者间的一致性。

Result: 显著性与可回答性之间呈稳健但低度的正相关，即更显著的问题更可能被回应；但该效应明显弱于独白文本，表明对话结构更不可预测；结构化互动中标注者间的对齐程度高于组织松散的对话。

Conclusion: QUD建模在自然对话中部分成立，但会话结构的可预测性有限；对话组织形式会影响标注一致性与模型的适用效果。

Abstract: We empirically investigate Question Under Discussion based modelling in naturalistic dialogue by studying whether the salience of generated potential questions predicts their subsequent resolution. Building on Wu et al. (2024), we construct a dataset of 7,124 questions automatically generated from utterances and preceding context from the British National Corpus, and annotated for salience and answerability. We find a robust but low positive correlation between salience and answerability in dialogue, indicating that more salient questions are more likely to be addressed. However, this effect is markedly weaker than in monologic text, suggesting that conversational structure is less predictable. We further observe that structured interactions exhibit stronger alignment between annotators than less organised dialogues.

</details>


### [48] [Improving Visual Sensitivity of LLMs on Multimodal Machine Translation with Metric-based Loss Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31169)
*Paweł Mąka,Piotr Andruszkiewicz,Yusuf Can Semerci,Jan Scholtes,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于度量的损失加权训练方法，通过PCXMI与一致性PCXMI识别受益于图像的token并加大其损失权重，提升多模态翻译的视觉敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态机器翻译虽引入图像等非文本模态以消解歧义，但模型在多模态融合后可能忽略图像信息，如何增强视觉敏感性仍是活跃问题。

Method: 提出Metric-based Loss Weighting训练方法：用Point-wise Cross-mutual Information（PCXMI）指标，比较有/无视觉上下文时模型的输出概率，识别受益于图像的token并提高其损失函数权重；同时提出Congruency-based PCXMI，两者结合使用。在三个翻译方向上对三个预训练多模态大语言模型进行图像引导机器翻译微调。

Result: 在CoMMuTE对比数据集上优于其他测试方法，相较标准微调准确率提升多达7个百分点以上，同时保持较强的通用翻译性能。

Conclusion: 基于度量的损失加权可有效提升多模态翻译的视觉落地与对比数据集准确率，且不损害通用翻译能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Machine Translation aims to incorporate additional signal from non-textual modalities to improve translations by resolving ambiguities. While models, through multimodal fusion, are able to accept images related to the source text, they can ignore this information. Therefore, increasing their visual sensitivity remains an active research area. In this work, we introduce a training method, Metric-based Loss Weighting, that improves visual grounding of translations by increasing the loss function for tokens that benefit from the accompanying image. We identify these tokens using the Point-wise Cross-mutual Information (PCXMI) metric, which compares the model's output probabilities with and without visual context. We introduce a Congruency-based PCXMI metric and experimentally show that both metrics working in combination yield the best results. We evaluate our method by fine-tuning three pretrained Multimodal Large Language Models on the task of Image-guided Machine Translation for three language directions. Metric-based Loss Weighting outperforms other tested methods on the CoMMuTE contrastive dataset, improving accuracy by up to more than 7 percentage points compared to standard fine-tuning, while maintaining strong general translation performance.

</details>


### [49] [Where a Model Sends Its Own Repeated Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31181)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文用退化输入（重复同一token）探测黑箱模型：对每个token t，读取 argmax p(.|t,t)，得到一个定义在整个词表上的映射。映射的"非不动点"部分此前未被记录，作者证明该部分可用于模型家族归因，并给出稳健性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱模型识别依赖对自然语言提示的响应打分；另有工作用退化输入（模型自身token重复）寻找失败模式而非身份特征。作者关注这一映射中此前被忽略的"非不动点"部分，试图从中提取可用于模型鉴别与归因的信号。

Method: 对每个token t作一次前向传播，取 argmax p(.|t,t)，构建词表上的映射，分为不动点集与非不动点集。以源token配对消除基数混杂；用频率匹配目的地与独立边缘分布作零假设对照；比较家族与分词器预测一致性；在7个分词器组、多语料、12个模型对19个模型池上评估，并做量化鲁棒性测试（8-bit/4-bit权重舍入、去重训练语料）。

Result: 不动点部分作为估计量失败：自然距离83%为基数，语料操纵仅以2比特区分（3471 vs 精度下限0），家族归因0.5833。非不动点部分配对后基数混杂被消除（r 从0.9128降至-0.0932），家族归因0.8333，随机基线0.1389；零假设对照分别为0.1429与0.0798。家族（0.2031）优于分词器（0.1205）预测一致性；循环架构平衡准确率1.0（多数类基线0.7895），排除主导目的地后为0.90。8-bit舍入影响小于语料去重（0.9004 vs 0.6353），4-bit破坏映射（0.0098；部署粒度0.1812）；精度下限随模型在0.201–0.9778间变化。

Conclusion: 退化输入映射的非不动点部分是稳健的模型家族归因信号，配对设计有效去除基数混杂，且优于频率与边缘零假设。作者主张对失败的估计量与存活估计量同等完整报告，并已预先注册所有估计量与kill条件。

Abstract: Black-box model identification works by scoring a model's response to natural-language prompts. One line of work feeds models a degenerate input -- their own token, repeated -- to find a failure mode rather than an identity. We take that input and ask where the model goes when it does not. For each token t, read argmax p(. | t, t) in one forward pass; the result is a map on the whole vocabulary, with two halves. The first -- which tokens are fixed points -- is partially anticipated, and we report it as a failed estimand: the natural distance on it is 83% cardinality, separates a corpus manipulation by two bits in 3471 against a precision floor of zero, and attributes families at 0.5833. The second half, where the map sends tokens that are not fixed points, is unrecorded; the one paper holding those tokens logged them as a zero. Pairing on the source token removes the cardinality confound by construction (r from 0.9128 to -0.0932) and attributes families at 0.8333 -- twelve models scored against a pool of nineteen -- with chance 0.1389, across seven tokenizer groups and several corpora. Two nulls clear it: frequency-matched destinations agree at 0.1429, independent marginals at 0.0798. Family predicts agreement better than tokenizer (0.2031 against 0.1205), and recurrent architectures cluster at balanced accuracy 1.0 against a 0.7895 majority rate, or 0.90 once each model's dominant destination is excluded -- the figure we stand behind. We measure the robustness envelope: 8-bit weight rounding moves the map less than deduplicating the training corpus does (0.9004 against 0.6353, on one support), 4-bit destroys it (0.0098; 0.1812 at deployment granularity, so not a coarseness artefact), and the precision floor varies by model from 0.201 to 0.9778. All estimands and kill conditions were registered before the data, and the failed one is reported as fully as the surviving one.

</details>


### [50] [RupeeBias: Auditing Demographic Bias in Indian Economic Guidance from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31245)
*Pavithra P M Nair,Bhavik Talaviya,Shourya Bhushan,Rahul Pankajakshan,Seema Guruvadoo,Avinash Agarwal,Gilad Gressel,Krishnashree Achuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RupeeBias，一个面向印度经济场景的人口统计偏差审计基准，涵盖39,150条提示、四种经济用例、六大人口统计轴与87个印度本地化标识，并在英印混用双语下评估九个LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体日益依赖LLM获取经济决策建议（如贷款比较、储蓄规划、薪资谈判与服务定价），而LLM已知会复现社会偏见，可能扭曲用户对自身价值的认知与索要、接受的金额。该风险在印度尤为突出，因为其经济结果受种姓、城乡等人口类别影响。然而现有基准多围绕西方人口类别设计，遗漏了印度经济差异的关键维度。

Method: 提出基准RupeeBias：包含39,150条提示，覆盖四个用例（薪资估算、薪资增幅估算、还价建议、服务定价建议）。采用单属性反事实设计，固定用户资历、经验或服务描述，仅逐一改变一个人口统计标识。覆盖87个印度特定标识、六大轴（种姓、宗教、地域身份、性别、残障、城乡），提示均以英语和Hinglish构建，并用于评估九个LLM。

Result: 在RupeeBias上评估九个LLM，发现六大轴均存在系统性人口统计差异；对仅人口标识不同的其他方面完全相同的提示，LLM生成的经济输出平均相差20.2%。

Conclusion: RupeeBias公开释放，以支持未来针对印度特定人口与经济情境下LLM经济建议中的统计偏差研究。

Abstract: Individuals turn to large language models (LLMs) for guidance across a wide range of economic tasks, from comparing loan options and planning savings to deciding what raise to ask for or how much to charge for their services. LLMs are known to reproduce social biases, and biased economic guidance may influence what users believe they are worth, what they ask for, and what they ultimately accept. This risk is especially salient in India, where economic outcomes are shaped by demographic categories such as caste and urban-rural location. Existing LLM bias benchmarks, however, are largely designed around Western demographic categories and therefore miss key axes of economic disparity in the Indian context. We introduce RupeeBias, a benchmark for auditing demographic bias in LLM-generated economic guidance across Indian economic settings. RupeeBias consists of 39,150 prompts spanning four use cases: salary estimation, salary increment estimation, counter-offer recommendation, and service pricing recommendation. The benchmark follows a single-attribute counterfactual design, holding the description of the user's qualifications, experience, or service offering fixed while varying one demographic identifier at a time. RupeeBias covers 87 India-specific demographic identifiers across six axes: caste, religion, regional identity, gender, disability, and urban-rural location, with all prompts constructed in both English and Hinglish. We evaluate nine LLMs on RupeeBias and find systematic demographic disparities across all six axes. For otherwise identical prompts that differ only in demographic identifier, LLM-generated economic outputs differ by 20.2% on average. We publicly release RupeeBias to support future research on demographic bias in LLM-generated economic guidance across India-specific demographic and economic contexts.

</details>


### [51] [PIA: A Personal Intelligence Agent Turning Health Conversations into Records and Records into Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31255)
*Jeonghun Yoon,Dongchan Kim,Hongyeon Yu,Young-Bum Kim,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PIA——与消费级健康智能体协同部署的个人智能体，通过可插拔健康模块的四项记忆控制（抽取、记忆、检索、理解），将对话转化为类型化临床记录并生成用户综合理解，实现从一维召回、二维健康快照到带因果的三维轨迹的分层记忆注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体记忆依赖摘要：抽取要点、嵌入、取 top-k 注入提示。但健康场景下该范式失效——剂量被压缩成句子、"since last week" 由模型任意解析、三个月血糖趋势无法用文本相似度回答。

Method: PIA 接收健康智能体的自然语言请求，自主决定是否及如何读写；其记忆骨架含四项域无关控制机制：抽取、记忆、检索、理解，各自配可插拔健康模块（schema、医学别名词典、知识图谱、时序规则），把对话转成类型化临床记录，再把记录合成为对用户的综合理解。

Result: 同一查询随注入上下文的记忆加深而得到不同答案：从一维召回，到二维健康快照，到带因果的三维轨迹。运营经验：自述健康数据非随机缺失；提问措辞决定综合理解质量；近三分之一的候选因果链接为结构性噪声，仅靠规则即可剔除。

Conclusion: 面向健康领域的智能体记忆需要结构化、类型化与时序化的机制，而非通用摘要式记忆；分层记忆注入能显著提升回答的临床深度，且规则化的健康模块在数据清洗与因果筛选上不可替代。

Abstract: General-purpose agent memory summarizes conversations: it extracts salient snippets, embeds them, and retrieves the top-k into the prompt. A health agent cannot run on summaries: a dose becomes a sentence, "since last week" is resolved at the model's discretion, and a three-month glucose trend cannot be answered by text similarity. We present PIA, a personal intelligence agent deployed alongside a consumer health agent. PIA receives the agent's natural-language requests, decides for itself whether and how to write or read, and turns conversations into typed clinical records and records into a synthesized understanding of the user. Its memory harness consists of four controls -- extraction, memory, retrieval, and understanding -- each a domain-agnostic mechanism with a pluggable health module: schema, medical alias dictionary, knowledge graph, and temporal rules. We show how the same query receives a different answer as the memory injected into the response context deepens from one-dimensional recall, to a two-dimensional health snapshot, to a three-dimensional trajectory with causality, and report lessons from operation: self-reported health data are missing not at random, question phrasing governs the quality of synthesized understanding, and nearly a third of candidate causal links are structural noise that rules alone remove.

</details>


### [52] [MoSAR: Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes for Learning Adaptive and Approximable Attention Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31261)
*Michele Paolicelli,Alessandro Petruzzelli,Alessandro Franceso Maria Martina,Cataldo Musto,Giovanni Semeraro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MoSAR将注意力近似重构为几何问题，通过输入条件路由学习自适应受控衰减的注意力几何，在降低注意力作用范围的同时匹配密集基线质量，并在长度外推上取得最优困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自注意力的二次复杂度是长上下文建模的核心瓶颈。现有高效方法往往预先规定稀疏或局部模式，但自然语言依赖是输入相关的、难以事先给定，因此应让模型从数据中学习交互几何，即哪些位置相关性可以衰减、哪些更广交互必须保留。

Method: 提出Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes（MoSAR）：在位置编码之后施加输入条件的query与key路由器，选择短程、中程、全局三种regime的混合，从而诱导连续的、距离相关的注意力场，而非固定稀疏模式。该几何在训练中学习，并可通过top-1路由离散化。

Result: 在匹配的500M参数模型对照预训练中，MoSAR学到作用范围显著更低的注意力几何，且不损害语言建模质量，在训练上下文长度上困惑度优于密集RoPE；在长度外推下，其困惑度在所有评测变体中最佳，包括ALiBi等强基线。所学习的几何在确定性top-1离散化下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 注意力近似应被视为可学习的几何问题。MoSAR表明输入条件、连续衰减的注意力几何既具有自适应性，又可在推理时被低成本近似，为长上下文建模提供了兼具质量与效率的路径。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of dense self-attention remains a central bottleneck for long-context language modeling. Many efficient alternatives address this cost by deciding in advance where attention should be sparse or local. We argue that attention approximation should instead be approached as a geometric problem, with the relevant interaction geometry learned from data: natural-language dependencies are input-dependent and difficult to prescribe in advance, so the model should learn where positional relevance can decay and where broader interactions must be preserved. We introduce Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes (MoSAR), which learns such an adaptive, controlled-decay geometry over query--key interactions. Input-conditioned query and key routers, applied after positional encoding, select mixtures over short, medium, and global regimes, inducing a continuous distance-dependent attention field rather than a fixed sparsity pattern. This geometry is learned during training and can subsequently be discretized through top-1 routing. In controlled pre-training experiments with matched 500M-parameter models, MoSAR learns a substantially lower-reach attention geometry without degrading language-modeling quality, improving perplexity over dense RoPE at the training context length. Under length extrapolation, MoSAR achieves the best perplexity among all evaluated variants, including strong baselines such as ALiBi. Moreover, the learned geometry remains stable under deterministic top-1 discretization, suggesting that it is not only adaptive, but also amenable to low-cost approximation at inference time.

</details>


### [53] [Identifying Scientists on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31264)
*Philipp Meier,Katarina Boland,Laura Kallmeyer,Stefan Dietze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在X/Twitter上自动识别科学家与非科学家账户的方法，基于用户简介与推文，采用随机森林与对比微调DeBERTa模型，F1分别达0.88与0.96，并发布两个标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络科学相关话语日益重要，经典知识秩序受到侵蚀，因此自动识别科学家等不同用户群体具有重要意义。

Method: 以X/Twitter用户的个人简介（user biographies）和推文为输入特征，一方面使用结合语言特征的随机森林分类器，另一方面采用对比式微调的DeBERTa模型并构建集成（ensemble）方案进行分类。

Result: 在两个不同数据集上均可将账户分类为科学家与非科学家：随机森林（含语言特征）最高F1为0.88，对比微调DeBERTa集成方案最高F1达0.96。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了基于用户简介与推文自动识别科学家的可行性，并提供两个标注了科学家/非科学家标签及对应推文与简介的X用户数据集，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: With the growing importance of science-related discourse on the Web and the erosion of the classical knowledge order, it is important to identify different user groups, such as scientists, automatically. This work proposes an approach for identifying scientists and non- scientists on X/Twitter based on their user biographies and tweets. We show that we are able to classify accounts as scientists and non- scientists on two different datasets, reaching an F1 score of up to 0.88 using Random Forests with linguistic features and up to 0.96 using a contrastively fine-tuned DeBERTa model in an ensemble setup. Furthermore, we provide two datasets with X users labeled as scientists or non scientists and their respective tweets and user biographies.

</details>


### [54] [Stale-Document Poisoning: When Outdated Retrieval Overrides Correct Model Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31342)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Lukas Galke Poech,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文识别出RAG中的"过期文档投毒"失败模式：过时证据会让本可正确回答的模型出错，并提出基于时效性判断的选择性信任方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG被广泛用于解决知识过时问题，但检索仅在证据仍然有效时才有帮助。论文指出一个被忽视的时间对齐失败——过期文档投毒：模型在无检索时能答对，却因检索到过时证据而答错。核心问题在于模型未能判断检索证据是否仍然适用。

Method: 构建包含317个经核实知识反转的基准，覆盖医学、法律、软件与平台政策，基于有日期的官方来源；在12个模型上评测投毒率，并对比匹配的最新证据被遵循的比例。为隔离时间适用性，固定50个反转的历史证据不变、仅改变评估日期。通过因果干预验证有效性信息对最终决策的作用；并评估固定的事实时效感知混合重排器在日期准确时的效果。

Result: 过时检索在无信任指令下仍使Llama翻转30%、Qwen翻转37%的答案，加入明确遵循指令后升至66%和75%；4个开源模型、4个领域中投毒率为17%-91%，而匹配的最新证据被遵循率97%-100%。仅改变日期带来的适应有限，但明确告知旧证据失效时点后，较大模型几乎能完美切换到正确回答。因果干预证实有效性信息直接影响最终决策，且相关内部组件也支持更广泛的比较任务。时效感知混合重排器在日期准确时将投毒降低4.6-10.0个百分点，收益依赖可靠的时间元数据。

Conclusion: 可靠的RAG需要"选择性信任"：模型不仅要判断检索证据说了什么，还要判断它是否仍然适用。时间适用性判断可复用通用的比较推理机制，而缓解效果依赖准确的时间元数据与重排策略。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is often used to address outdated knowledge by providing external evidence. But retrieval helps only when that evidence is still valid. We identify a temporal alignment failure, stale-document poisoning, in which outdated evidence makes a model wrong despite answering correctly without retrieval. We construct a benchmark of 317 verified knowledge reversals across medicine, law, software, and platform policy, grounded in dated official sources. Across 12 models, recent medical reversals are harder than long-established ones. More importantly, outdated retrieval flips 30% of Llama and 37% of Qwen answers even without instructions to trust the document; explicit follow instructions raise these rates to 66% and 75%. Across four open models and four domains, poisoning ranges from 17-91%, while matched up-to-date evidence is followed in 97-100% of trials. To isolate temporal applicability, we keep the historical evidence unchanged across 50 reversals and vary only the evaluation date. A clear pattern emerges: dates alone produce only modest adaptation, but when models are explicitly told when the old evidence stops applying, the larger models switch to the appropriate answer almost perfectly. Causal interventions confirm that this validity information directly shapes the final decision. The same internal components also support broader comparison tasks, suggesting that temporal applicability can recruit a general reasoning mechanism used for other comparisons. Finally, a fixed recency-aware hybrid re-ranker reduces poisoning by 4.6-10.0 points when dates are accurate, with gains that depend on reliable temporal metadata. Reliable RAG therefore requires selective trust: models must determine not only what retrieved evidence says, but whether it still applies.

</details>


### [55] [Highlight-Then-Summarize: Learning to Compress Evidence for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31382)
*Zhaoyuan Xia,Qinghongbing Xie,Yung Xiang Hue,Jianguang Jiang,Gaofeng Lu,Zhenyu Jiao,Xing Yuan,Dai Dai,Tong Mo,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Highlight-Then-Summarize (H2S)，一种"先压缩再推理"范式：先定位与问题相关的源证据，再整合为紧凑的、以问题为条件的摘要，最后生成答案；配套H2S-Dataset与H2S-RL训练，在长上下文基准上超过更大规模开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文理解中，任务相关证据往往稀疏且分散在大量无关、冗余内容之中，直接推理效率与效果受限，因此需要显式地筛选并整合证据。

Method: 采用"压缩-再推理"的H2S范式：第一步识别有源文档支撑、与问题相关的证据，第二步将其整合为紧凑、以问题为条件的摘要，再据此生成答案。为训练该行为，构建H2S-Dataset（11个基准家族、6,647个样本，平均上下文43.9K tokens），并提出H2S-RL，在最终答案正确性之外，为证据选择与摘要构建提供过程级奖励。

Result: 在七任务长上下文套件H2S-Bench上，统一128K输入、4K输出预算下，H2S-14B平均得分32.60，超过Qwen3.8-27B 10.17分，为所评估开源模型中最优；同时取得最高Evidence-Summary Quality分数，并在仅4K输出预算下保留其16K预算性能的97.1%。

Conclusion: 显式地选择与整合证据能够提升长上下文推理能力，并支持更紧凑的生成。

Abstract: Long-context understanding requires large language models (LLMs) to reason over lengthy documents, conversations, and code, yet task-relevant evidence is often sparse and scattered amid substantial irrelevant and redundant content. We propose Highlight-Then-Summarize (H2S), a compress-then-reason paradigm that first identifies source-grounded, question-relevant evidence and then integrates it into a compact, question-conditioned summary before producing the final answer. To train this behavior, we construct H2S-Dataset, comprising 6,647 examples from 11 benchmark families with an average context length of 43.9K tokens, and introduce H2S-RL, which provides process-level rewards for evidence selection and summary construction in addition to final-answer correctness. We evaluate on H2S-Bench, a seven-task long-context suite. Under a shared 128K input and 4K output budget, H2S-14B achieves an average score of 32.60, outperforming Qwen3.8-27B by 10.17 points and obtaining the strongest overall result among the evaluated open-source models. H2S-14B also achieves the highest Evidence-Summary Quality score and retains 97.1% of its 16K-budget performance with only a 4K output budget. These results show that explicitly selecting and integrating evidence improves long-context reasoning while enabling more compact generation.

</details>


### [56] [Sorry Robot, Happy Human: Vision-Language Models Read Only One of Two Legible Typographic Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31403)
*Mert İncidelen,Yamen Kashkash,Asya Berker,Murat Aydoğan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出自动生成多层字体攻击数据的自动化流水线Decoy Font，构建DecoyBench基准（300张图像），评估6个闭源VLM：高分辨率下可读出轮廓文字但几乎无法读出阴影文字，低分辨率下人类与模型均无法读轮廓文字却能高精度读出阴影文字，揭示VLM处理多空间频率层文字时的系统性行为局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM虽在OCR任务中表现优异，却易受排版攻击（typographic attacks），且对包含多层文字的图像结构鲁棒性差。作者希望系统刻画这种脆弱性，并排除数据集人工设计带来的偏差，因此需要一种可控、可扩展的攻击图像生成方式与配套评测基准。

Method: 提出Decoy Font方法：自动生成"诱饵字体"图像，将清晰轮廓线文字叠加在柔和阴影文字之上，形成多空间频率层结构。基于该方法构建DecoyBench（300张图像）。选取来自3个模型家族的6个近期闭源模型，在2种提示条件（naive与guided）和2种分辨率（512×512与64×64）下进行评测，并开展人类被试验证研究作为对照。

Result: 人类被试能以高准确率读出两层文字。高分辨率下，多数模型变体在两种提示方法下均以接近人类水平读出轮廓文字，但几乎从未完整提取阴影文字；低分辨率下，模型与人类均无法读出轮廓文字，而阴影文字可被高精度提取。

Conclusion: 所评估的VLM在处理含多个空间频率层的排版结构时表现出一致的行为局限：模型倾向于锁定某一空间频率层的文字而忽略另一层，且该现象跨模型家族、提示方式与分辨率稳定存在，说明这是模型层面的结构性缺陷，而非提示设计或单一模型问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), despite their success in optical character recognition (OCR) tasks, are vulnerable to typographic attacks and have a fragile structure for images with multiple text layers. In this study, the DecoyBench dataset was created using the Decoy Font method. The dataset consists of 300 images, each containing text with sharp contour lines superimposed on another text with soft shading. Six recent closed-source models from three different model families were evaluated using this dataset under two different prompting conditions (naive and guided) and at two different resolutions ($512\times512$ and $64\times64$). A validation study showed that human participants could read both text layers with high accuracy. In contrast, the models, with most variants and both prompting methods, read the contour text with near-human accuracy at high resolution, but almost never fully extracted the shading text. At low resolution, the contour text could not be read by either the models or humans, while the shading text could be extracted with high accuracy. The findings indicate that the evaluated VLMs exhibit a consistent behavioral limitation when processing typographic structures containing multiple spatial frequency layers.

</details>


### [57] [ViSTA: A Simple Bridge Extends Visual Alignment to Clinical Time-Series Understanding in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31448)
*Junyi Gao,Yu Shi,Pingzhao Hu,Ewen M Harrison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ViSTA适配器，将不规则数值测量注入预训练视觉-语言模型的图表表征中，仅训练极少参数即实现临床风险预测与时间问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型擅长基于结构化高维时间序列做风险估计，大语言模型擅长医学文本理解与问答，但后者的语言能力并不能保证从结构化临床时间序列中做出准确预测。若能将两者打通，就可把风险估计与关于患者病情演变的灵活提问结合起来。

Method: ViSTA是一个紧凑适配器，把不规则的数值测量整合进预训练视觉-语言模型的图表（chart）表征中：它学习对视觉token的修正量，同时保持所有预训练参数冻结不变。仅需约0.516M可训练参数。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV上，对急性肾损伤（AKI）和死亡率预测，ViSTA在2–9B参数各模型中四项指标的平均分均为对比适配方法中最高。2B模型以0.516M可训练参数在AKI上达到AUROC 0.7376，接近GPT-5.6 Sol用文本输入加高推理强度的0.7380。用于时间问答训练时，4B参数下取得69.27%准确率，可训练参数比使用图表或数值文本的低秩适配（LoRA）少90%以上，准确率差距仅2.82–4.88个百分点。

Conclusion: ViSTA把预训练语言模型的能力扩展到数值预测与时间问答任务上，以极低的可训练参数代价实现了接近甚至媲美大规模强模型的临床预测与问答性能。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models estimate risk from patient measurements, while large language models support medical text understanding and question answering. Yet their language capabilities do not ensure accurate prediction from structured, high-dimensional clinical time series. Improving this ability would connect risk estimation with flexible questions about a patient's evolving condition. We introduce ViSTA, a compact adapter that incorporates irregular numerical measurements into a pretrained vision-language model's chart representations. It learns corrections to visual tokens while leaving all pretrained parameters unchanged. On MIMIC-IV, ViSTA has the highest mean scores among the compared adaptations on all four metrics for acute kidney injury and mortality prediction across models with 2-9 billion parameters. With 0.516 million trainable parameters, the 2-billion-parameter model reaches an area under the ROC curve of 0.7376 for acute kidney injury, compared with GPT-5.6 Sol's 0.7380 with text input and high reasoning effort. Training for temporal question answering yields 69.27% accuracy at 4 billion parameters with over 90% fewer trainable parameters than low-rank adaptation using charts or numerical text, at a 2.82-4.88 percentage-point accuracy gap. ViSTA extends pretrained language models to numerical prediction and temporal questions.

</details>


### [58] [Evaluating Cultural Awareness of LLMs for Haitian Creole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31506)
*Christelle Clervilsson,Yanzhu Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估大语言模型在海地克里奥尔语上的文化意识，从特异性、偏见、多样性、变异四个维度出发，使用母语者构建的文化显著提示基准，发现其与高资源法语存在明显差距，且叙事中易固化刻板印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高资源与低资源语言间存在显著性能差异，且往往无法把握欠代表社群的文化规范与价值。海地克里奥尔语使用人口众多却在数字资源中严重不足，其文化意识此前缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建由母语者策划的文化显著提示基准，在文本填空（text infilling）设定下，从特异性、偏见、多样性、变异四个互补维度对LLM的文化意识进行系统评估，并与高资源法语进行对比，同时开展故事生成分析。

Result: 海地克里奥尔语的文化意识与法语存在明显差距，其表现跨领域更不均衡，并更易受法语语言干扰。故事生成显示海地角色反复被塑造成历经苦难与坚韧的形象，表明即便正面刻画也可能编码刻板化叙事。

Conclusion: 低资源语言的文化意识评估揭示了LLM在文化代表性上的系统性不足，负面与"正面"刻板印象并存。作者公开了代码、基准与评估框架，以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit substantial performance disparities between high- and low-resource languages. Beyond lower task performance, they often fail to capture the cultural norms and values of underrepresented communities. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of cultural awareness in LLMs for Haitian Creole, a language spoken by millions but severely underrepresented in digital resources. We assess cultural awareness along four complementary dimensions---specificity, bias, diversity, and variation---using a benchmark of culturally salient prompts curated by native speakers in a text infilling setting. Our results reveal a clear gap between cultural awareness in Haitian Creole and higher-resource French, with Haitian performance being more uneven across domains and more affected by French linguistic interference. Story generation further reveals recurring portrayals of Haitian characters through hardship and resilience, showing that even positive characterizations can encode stereotypical narratives. Our code, benchmark, and evaluation framework are publicly available.

</details>


### [59] [Muslim: A Deployed Arabic Voice AI Platform for Grounded Islamic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31511)
*Yahya Mohamed Elnawasany*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Muslim——一个面向真实用户、提供有据可依的伊斯兰知识的生产级阿拉伯语语音 AI 平台，并重点报告了原型系统通常欠缺的三类工程要素：已发布的微调模型产物、账号与计量层、以及三层可观测性体系，同时给出实测延迟与准确率数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究原型通常缺少从"开放演示"到"可运营产品"所需的关键要素：可发布的模型产物、滥用防护与计量机制、以及面向系统特征故障模式的运维监控。作者希望展示在真实生产环境中运行伊斯兰知识语音产品的完整工程实践与权衡。

Method: 系统由实时语音管线（NeMo 阿拉伯语 ASR、OpenAI 兼容的 LLM 端点、自托管 TTS）与确定性多源检索层构成，检索通过六个 Model Context Protocol 服务器进行路由。发布了两类微调模型产物：工具路由 LLM（Muslim-6B-PRO，5.94B 参数）与现代标准阿拉伯语 TTS 模型（Fasih-TTS-V1）。此外设计了账号与计量层（每账号免费轮次额度、容量感知拒答、延迟至必要时才进行邮箱验证）以及三层可观测性栈（存活检测、错误上报、产品分析），专门针对"GPU 受限的 agent 主机静默而 Web 层仍正常服务"这一特征故障模式。

Result: 在社区投票的阿拉伯语 TTS Arena（MSA）上，Fasih-TTS-V1 在 17 个系统中总排名第 5，在 11 个开放权重系统中排名第 2。实测指标包括：124 个案例上的诵读验证准确率达 98.4%，端到端语音延迟为 0.9–1.7 秒。

Conclusion: 论文展示了将伊斯兰知识语音产品投入生产的可行性，并讨论了具体的工程权衡与局限性。其贡献在于提供了原型常缺失的三类实践要素：可发布的微调模型、可运营的抗滥用计量层、以及针对特定故障模式定制的可观测性体系。

Abstract: We present Muslim, a production Arabic voice AI platform serving grounded, sourced Islamic knowledge to real users. Beyond a real-time voice pipeline (NeMo Arabic ASR, an OpenAI-compatible LLM endpoint, self-hosted TTS) and a deterministic multi-source retrieval layer routed across six Model Context Protocol servers, we report three things a research prototype typically lacks. First, a released family of fine-tuned Arabic Islamic model artifacts: an efficient tool-routing LLM (Muslim-6B-PRO, 5.94B parameters) and a Modern Standard Arabic TTS model (Fasih-TTS-V1) that ranks 5th of 17 overall and 2nd of 11 open-weight systems on the community-voted Arabic TTS Arena for MSA. Second, an account and metering layer - a free per-account turn allowance, capacity-aware refusal, and email verification deferred to the point it actually matters - that turns an open demo into an operable, abuse-resistant product. Third, a three-layer observability stack (liveness, error reporting, product analytics) built specifically around the system's characteristic failure mode: a GPU-bound agent host going silent while the web tier keeps serving normally. We report real, measured latency and accuracy figures (98.4% recitation-validation accuracy on 124 cases; end-to-end voice latency of 0.9-1.7s) and discuss the concrete engineering trade-offs and limitations of running an Islamic-knowledge voice product in production.

</details>


### [60] [MexHat: A Dataset for Hate Speech Detection in Mexican Spanish Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31553)
*Itzel Tlelo-Coyotecatl,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MexHat，一个面向墨西哥西班牙语语境的视频仇恨言论检测数据集，包含约1000个视频片段，支持三分类与细粒度子类标注，并提供基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测在内容安全中日益重要，且本质上依赖语境线索来准确理解内容意图。然而非英语资源稀缺，现有模型难以适应多模态内容中微妙、依赖语境且与文化相关的特性，尤其缺乏面向墨西哥西班牙语语境的资源。

Method: 构建 MexHat 视频数据集，聚焦墨西哥西班牙语的语言与文化线索。数据集包含约1k个视频片段，进行两类标注任务：一是三分类评估（无负面内容、冒犯性内容、仇恨言论内容）；二是细粒度评估，包含三个仇恨言论子类别。

Result: 通过数据集统计与基线实验结果，揭示了该任务本身固有的挑战性。

Conclusion: MexHat 填补了墨西哥西班牙语多模态仇恨言论检测资源的空白，为后续语境与文化敏感的检测研究提供了数据基础与基线参考。论文包含可能令部分读者不适的敏感内容。

Abstract: Ensuring online safety through content monitoring had raised Hate Speech Detection as a crucial task to be addressed. By essence the task demands the capture of contextual cues, which are essential for a precise understanding of the content's intent. Although automated detection approaches for the task have advanced significantly, the scarcity of non-English resources persists, limiting the ability of models to adapt to the subtle, context-dependent, and culturally related nature of multimodal content. In this paper, we introduce MexHat, a video dataset designed to capture the linguistic and cultural cues for the hate-speech detection task in a Mexican Spanish context. Our dataset comprises around 1k video clips annotated across two tasks: a three-way class evaluation (no negative content, offensive content and hate-speech content), and a fine-grained class evaluation including three hate-speech sub-categories. The dataset statistics and the baseline results highlight the inherent challenges associated with the task. Disclaimer: This paper contains sensitive content that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [61] [Strategically Diverse Sampling for Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31571)
*Alexander Gurung,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出以"策略多样性"而非"正确性"作为自训练数据构建准则，通过GROOT（层次化方法树采样）与Verbalized Sampling两种方法生成策略多样数据，在竞技编程和NCP任务上超越IID数据训练，且策略多样但错误的轨迹可超过235B教师蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练与推理方法（RL、test-time scaling）依赖重复采样，但仅当响应存在实质差异时才有收益。自训练通常采样IID响应并主要按正确性过滤，导致模型偏好的策略被过度代表，缺乏策略层面的多样性。作者因此探究"策略多样性"作为构建自训练数据的替代原则。

Method: 提出策略多样性概念并设计两种采样方法：GROOT——构建问题解决方法的分层树并采样不同路径；Verbalized Sampling（VS）——适配为生成无结构的方法集合。在竞技编程与Next-Chapter Prediction两个领域，用策略采样数据训练模型，并与IID训练、RL、test-time scaling初始化及235B教师蒸馏进行对比。

Result: 在困难任务上，策略采样数据训练的模型优于IID训练的对应模型，并为RL与test-time scaling提供强初始化。最显著的是，用Qwen3-4B生成的策略多样但错误的轨迹做自训练，效果超过235B教师的IID蒸馏。

Conclusion: 结果挑战了关于自训练数据有效性的主流假设，表明策略多样性可能比正确性或教师规模更重要，为自训练数据构建提供了新原则。

Abstract: Many LLM training and inference methods, including RL and test-time scaling, depend on repeated sampling, but benefit only when the responses meaningfully differ. Self-training faces the same challenge: training data is typically constructed by sampling IID responses and filtering primarily for correctness, thereby overrepresenting strategies a model already favours. We investigate strategic diversity, or substantive variation among approaches to a problem, as an alternative principle for constructing self-training data. We generate strategically diverse data with two sampling methods: GROOT, a new method which constructs a hierarchical tree of approaches and samples distinct paths, and Verbalized Sampling (VS), adapted to produce an unstructured set of approaches. Across competitive programming and Next-Chapter Prediction domains, models trained on strategically sampled data outperform IID-trained counterparts on difficult tasks and provide strong initializations for RL and test-time scaling. Most strikingly, self-training on strategically diverse but incorrect traces from Qwen3-4B outperforms IID distillation from a 235B teacher. These results challenge prevailing assumptions about what makes useful self-training data and show that diversity of approaches can matter more than correctness or teacher scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [Pretrained ASR Pseudo-labeling for Noisy Police Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30469)
*Kaavya Chaparala,Su Huang,Stephen L. Miller,Rhiannon N. Miller,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了伪标签在极噪的警务广播通信（BPC）域上适配基础ASR模型（Whisper与Qwen3-ASR）的机会与局限，发现内部置信度指标无法区分伪标签质量，提出基于LLM-as-a-judge的外部过滤范式及跨模型伪标签范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练ASR系统在嘈杂的警务广播通信（BPC）上表现不佳，阻碍了对警务决策的理解；伪标签提供了一条无需昂贵人工标注的无监督改进路径，但其在极噪域上的有效性尚不清楚。因此需要系统评估伪标签在该域上的机会与局限，并探索更有效的伪标签质量筛选方法。

Method: 以Whisper和Qwen3-ASR基础模型为对象，在巴尔的摩和芝加哥的BPC语料上进行伪标签适配的实验。首先检验现有内部置信度指标（对数概率与STAR分数）区分伪标签质量的能力；进而提出外部LLM-as-a-judge过滤范式，利用参数化知识剔除上下文不合理转写；并提出跨模型伪标签范式，即用一个模型的伪标签微调另一个模型。

Result: 现有内部置信度指标（对数概率与STAR分数）无法区分BPC伪标签的高质量与低质量；LLM-judge过滤比内部指标更激进，显著降低了巴尔的摩和芝加哥BPC语料上伪标签训练集的WER，但与oracle过滤相比仍存在明显差距；跨模型伪标签被识别为未来伪标签工作的有前景方向。

Conclusion: 在极噪BPC域上，内部置信度指标不足以筛选伪标签质量，外部LLM-as-a-judge过滤能显著提升伪标签训练集质量但未达oracle水平；跨模型伪标签是值得进一步探索的方向。

Abstract: Pretrained ASR systems perform poorly on noisy Broadcast Police Communication (BPC), hindering efforts to understand police decision-making. Pseudo-labeling offers an unsupervised path to improve ASR without expensive human labels, but the efficacy of this approach on very noisy domains is not known. In this work, we systematically assess the opportunities and limits of pseudo-labeling to adapt foundation ASR models (Whisper and Qwen3-ASR) to noisy BPC domain corpora from Baltimore and Chicago. We demonstrate that existing internal confidence metrics (log-probabilities and STAR scores) fail to distinguish between high and low quality BPC pseudo-labels, and we introduce an external LLM-as-a-judge filtering paradigm that leverages parametric knowledge to discard contextually implausible transcripts. Our LLM-judging filters more aggressively than internal metrics and significantly reduces WER of the pseudo-labeled training sets across the Baltimore and Chicago BPC corpora, though a substantial gap remains relative to an oracle filter. We also introduce a new cross-model pseudo-labeling paradigm where one model is finetuned with pseudo-labels from the other, and we identify this method as a promising direction for future pseudo-labeling work.

</details>


### [63] [Do LLMs Understand Context? A Knowledge Graph-Based Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30484)
*Subavarshana Arumugam,Mamta Nallaretnam,Kithuni Wickramasinghe,Chamath Gunapala,Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Uthayasanker Thayasivam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于知识图谱的LLM上下文理解评估框架，核心是融合结构与语义信号的混合相似度度量S3KG，并构建三元组级诊断分析框架，在九个基准上取得最高+7.6的F1提升，AUROC达0.973。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽具备强大语言能力，但其究竟是否真正理解上下文，还是仅在大规模上进行模式匹配，尚存疑问。传统评估方法如BLEU和困惑度仅衡量表层性能，无法反映问答中答案是否真正基于上下文，而非记忆关联，导致评估存在关键缺口。

Method: 提出一种基于知识图谱的LLM上下文理解评估框架。核心为S3KG（Semantic Structural Similarity for KGs），一种将结构与语义信号融合为单一分数的混合相似度度量。同时开发诊断分析框架，在三元组层面识别并归类推理错误，实现对模型失败的细粒度分析。

Result: 在九个基准上，S3KG相比最强基线实现最高+7.6个百分点的F1提升，AUROC最高达0.973。

Conclusion: 该工作通过知识图谱驱动的混合相似度度量与三元组级错误诊断，弥补了传统表层指标在评估LLM上下文理解方面的不足，为问答场景下模型上下文接地能力提供了更细粒度、更有效的评估手段。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable linguistic capabilities, a profound question lingers at their core: do these models truly comprehend context or simply excel at pattern matching on an unprecedented scale? Contextual understanding in LLMs refers to the ability to correctly extract relevant information from a given context, integrate it into a coherent internal representation, and reason over it to produce factually consistent and contextually grounded responses. However, traditional methods such as BiLingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and perplexity simply measure surface-level performance. This reveals a critical gap in question answering (QA), where responses must be contextually grounded rather than simply being memorized associations. To fill this void, we propose a novel knowledge graph (KG) based evaluation framework for LLM contextual understanding in QA. Central to this is Semantic Structural Similarity for KGs (S3KG), a hybrid similarity measure combining structural and semantic signals into a single score. In addition, a diagnostic analysis framework is developed to identify and categorize reasoning errors at the triplet level, enabling fine-grained analysis of model failures. Together, across nine benchmarks, S3KG achieves F1 gains of up to $+7.6$ points over the strongest baseline and AUROC up to $0.973$.

</details>

*Shun Ye,Vinny Chandran Suja,Chenlong Li,Chongming Jiang,Reza Zamani,Xiang Li,Christopher Bain,Yuqi Zhou,Walker Peterson,Huidong Wang,Chenglang Hu,Jongchan Park,Xiao Cheng,Benjamin Swedlund,Sandra Murillo,Anjali Sivanandan,Shiyu Sun,Liang Lanfeng,Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Baju C. Joy,Ishaq N. Khan,Sreedhar S. Kumar,Gabriel Mercado-Vásquez,James V. Vizzard,Jonathan M. Matthews,Helen Huang,Xiaolu Guo,Ethan Nicklow,Guorui Chen,Ryan A. Neff,Surjendu Maity,Hyeonjin Park,Han-ho Joo,Katherine Dong,Yuyan Cai,Weihang Huang,Yichen Zou,Rui Yan,Raphael Figueroa,Artem Goncharov,Bella Rose Schremmer,Lian Elsa Linton,Keisuke Goda,Liang Gao,Ke Cheng,Leonardo Morsut,Jennifer L. Wilson,Jianping Fu,Lim Chwee Teck,Deblina Sarkar,Andreas Hierlemann,Savaş Tay,Alexander Hoffmann,Donald Richieri Griffin,Jun Chen,Shana O. Kelley,Shyni Varghese,Jinwoo Cheon,Wilbur A. Lam,James J. Moon,Wilson W. Wong,Samir Mitragotri,Dino Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioEVAL 是一个全球多机构共建的生物工程（BE）博士级基准，包含 608 个评测项（359 道保留的 MCQ、218 个文献综述任务、10 个多模态题），用于评估 LLM 的实验推理能力。最高 MCQ 准确率约 90%、文献综述相似度 0.72、多模态准确率约 80%，各子领域差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 基准偏重事实性回忆，难以刻画模型在前沿与多模态任务上的表现，尤其缺乏针对生物工程实验推理能力的系统评估，因此需要一个跨机构、博士级、面向实验推理的基准来定位当前能力、局限与研发优先方向。

Method: 由 22 个研究组共建 BioEVAL，覆盖 11 个生物工程子领域及未分类项，共 608 个博士级评测项：380 道 MCQ（审计后保留 359 道）、218 个文献综述任务、10 个需实验图像解读的多模态问题。题目先经作者组专家评审与集中质量控制；评测后再由盲审跨组共识审计最高/最低准确率的 MCQ，剔除或标注 21 题，所有报告结果基于保留的 359 题。评测对象包括云侧基础/多模态模型（如 ChatGPT、Gemini、Grok）及可在消费级 GPU 本地部署的模型。

Result: MCQ 最高准确率约 90%；文献综述相似度得分 0.72；小样本多模态推理准确率约 80%；不同子领域间性能差异显著。排行榜揭示了各 BE 任务类别上的当前能力、局限与发展优先项。

Conclusion: BioEVAL 作为可扩展基准，配合标准化协议，支持持续性的专家题目贡献与模型评测，为生物工程领域 LLM 的实验推理能力提供可复用的评估框架与方向指引。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated historic breakthroughs in general reasoning with early successes in biomedical science. However, existing LLM benchmarking emphasizes factual recall, offering limited insight into model performance on frontier and multimodal tasks. We assembled BioEVAL (BioEngineering Validation of AI and LLMs), a global, multi-institutional initiative designed to assess experimental reasoning capability across bioengineering (BE) subfields. BioEVAL spans 11 major BE subfields plus a set of uncategorized items, bringing together 22 research groups to create a PhD-level benchmark comprising 608 evaluation items: 1) 380 multiple-choice questions (MCQs, 359 retained after audit), 2) 218 literature synthesis tasks, and 3) 10 multimodal problems with experimental image interpretation. Benchmark items underwent authoring-group expert review and centralized quality control before evaluation. Following evaluation, a blinded cross-group consensus audit of the highest- and lowest-accuracy MCQ items flagged 21 questions for revision or removal; these were withheld, and all reported MCQ results are computed on the 359 retained items. We evaluated diverse cloud-scale foundation/multimodal models (e.g., ChatGPT, Gemini, and Grok) and locally deployable models suitable for inference on consumer-grade GPUs. Models achieved the highest accuracy of up to 90% on MCQs, similarity score of 0.72 on literature synthesis, and accuracy of 80% on a small sample of multimodal reasoning questions, with substantial performance variation across subfields. Leaderboard rankings characterize current capabilities, limitations, and development priorities across the evaluated BE task categories. BioEVAL is maintained as an extensible benchmark with standardized protocols for continuing expert item contribution and model evaluation.

</details>


### [65] [Benchy: towards a universal language for task-oriented AI benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30550)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Benchy 是一种用于 AI 程序基准测试的语义语言与执行引擎，将基准定义为 B=(P,S,D) 并与被测 AI 系统分离，运行 R=(B,AI) 将两者绑定；基准以规范化 YAML 编写，经本体分类后确定性编译为规范化 JSON 中间表示，由引擎执行，并对外提供统一运行时契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试常将基准定义与 AI 系统实现耦合，集成机制易渗透进基准语义，且定义语法不唯一、存在隐式默认与自动修复，导致语义不透明、可复现性差。作者希望把基准作为独立、可确定编译与执行的语义对象来规范。

Method: 1) 语义对象模型：基准 B=(P,S,D) 由程序、评分函数、数据集完全指定，与 AI 系统分离；运行 R=(B,AI) 绑定二者。2) 基准以规范化 YAML 编写，每个语义概念仅对应一种合法语法，按共享的任务/领域/语言本体分类。3) 确定性编译为规范化 JSON 中间表示；编译只改变表示、不改变含义，不修复非法定义、不注入隐藏默认。4) 程序采用固定的命名输入/输出字段模式，叶子输出字段即评分维度。5) 引擎暴露单一通用运行时契约：命名字段输入对象入、命名字段输出对象出，外部 AI 系统在边界处适配。

Result: 论文给出了语义对象模型、本体与任务到程序的校验规则、评分与失败语义、编译与执行架构，以及当前语言的范围；附录固定了首个引擎实现的规范性工程契约。

Conclusion: Benchy 通过规范化语法、确定性编译与统一运行时契约，将基准语义与 AI 系统集成机制解耦，为 AI 程序基准测试提供了可确定、可复现的语义语言与执行引擎规范。

Abstract: Benchy is a semantic language and execution engine for benchmarking AI programs. A benchmark is completely specified by a program, a scoring function, and a dataset, B=(P,S,D), and is separate from the AI-system taking it; a run binds the two, R=(B,AI). Benchmarks are authored as canonical YAML in which each semantic concept has one valid syntax, classified by a shared task/domain/language ontology, and deterministically compiled into a canonical JSON intermediate representation that the engine executes. Compilation changes representation, not meaning: it does not repair invalid definitions or inject hidden defaults. Programs use fixed schemas of named input and output fields, the leaf output fields are the scoring dimensions, and the engine exposes one universal runtime contract --- a named-field input object in, a named-field output object out --- to which external AI-systems adapt at the boundary, so integration mechanics never propagate into benchmark semantics. This paper gives the semantic object model, the ontology and task-to-program validation rule, the scoring and failure semantics, the compilation and execution architecture, and the scope of the current language. An appendix fixes the normative engineering contract for the first engine implementation.

</details>


### [66] [Rank-Reliable Teacher-Guided Fitness Approximation for Expensive Evolutionary Optimization: A TinyML Architecture Search Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30553)
*Soumen Garai,Suman Samui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TGL-NSGA-II低精度框架，用教师引导的短程知识蒸馏评分与高斯过程代理融合，为受限TinyML神经架构搜索提供可靠的低成本候选排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昂贵的进化搜索并不总需要每个候选的精确适应度，而只需可靠回答“哪个候选更好”这一更简单的问题；同时TinyML架构搜索需在严格评估预算下保持排序与选择质量。

Method: 预训练教师按难度与类别联合分层组织样本；每个候选先经KD-Lite（在紧凑训练集上短程且有上限的知识蒸馏），再在独立分层评估集上打分；该教师引导分数与高斯过程代理融合以选择进入全评估的候选。理论分析覆盖评估方差、分数集中度、成对秩反转、期望Kendall-τ、首前沿识别与超体积扰动，并推导方差感知融合权重与容量自适应蒸馏规则。

Result: 在关键词唤醒与鸟鸣分类上，实测Kendall-τ为0.74与0.62，高于预测下界0.60与0.46；联合分层较随机评估将代理分数方差降低41%，而选择性教师错配会增大差异偏差并把Kendall-τ降至0.41。受限评估预算下，TGL-NSGA-II在关键词唤醒上取得最大平均超体积与最小世代距离，在BirdCLEF上取得最低平均假阳性率，且较完整NSGA-II快2.2倍。

Conclusion: 教师引导的低精度评分结合代理融合能在受限预算下有效提升TinyML架构搜索的排序可靠性与多目标性能，但相关保证仅适用于群体级低精度评估，不构成完整进化轨迹的收敛性证明。

Abstract: Expensive evolutionary search does not always need an exact fitness estimate for every candidate. It often needs a reliable answer to a simpler question: which candidate is better? We address this need through Teacher-Guided Learning NSGA-II (TGL-NSGA-II), a low-fidelity framework for constrained Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) neural architecture search. A pretrained teacher organizes samples into strata defined jointly by difficulty and class. Each candidate then undergoes KD-Lite, a short and capped knowledge-distillation procedure on a compact training set, before being scored on a separate stratified evaluation set. This teacher-guided score is fused with a Gaussian-process surrogate to select candidates for full evaluation. For a fixed candidate population, we analyse evaluation variance, score concentration, pairwise rank inversion, expected Kendall-$τ$, first-front identification, and hypervolume perturbation. We also derive a variance-aware fusion weight and a capacity-adaptive distillation rule. On keyword spotting and bird-call classification, the measured Kendall-$τ$ values are 0.74 and 0.62, exceeding the corresponding predicted lower bounds of 0.60 and 0.46. Joint stratification reduces proxy-score variance by 41% relative to random evaluation. Selective teacher mismatch, in contrast, increases differential bias and reduces Kendall-$τ$ to 0.41. Under a constrained evaluation budget, TGL-NSGA-II achieves the largest mean hypervolume and smallest generational distance on keyword spotting, records the lowest mean false-positive rate on BirdCLEF, and runs 2.2x faster than full NSGA-II. These guarantees apply to population-level low-fidelity evaluation and do not establish convergence of the complete evolutionary trajectory.

</details>


### [67] [Thinking Less to Simulate Better: Intuitive Prompting Improves LLM Agents Simulating Individual Social Media Reactions, Including Unfamiliar Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30563)
*Ljubisa Bojic,Tijana Stanic,Joerg Matthes,Agariadne Dwinggo Samala,Bojana Dinic,Jue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过8名塞尔维亚被试的问卷、深度访谈与书面自我陈述构建档案，记录其对68条社交媒体帖子的反应，并测试4种语言模型在5种提示条件下的预测表现。结果发现：态度性内容远优于人口学背景信息；智能体与自身档案的一致性高于被试与自身问卷答案的一致性；一旦提供档案信息，一致性与保真度无关；"直觉、即时"提示取得最高保真度，并将个体差异压缩从人力的7倍降至3倍，且在问卷未涉及的话题上仍保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台政策日益依赖人工用户进行测试，智能体的保真度因此变得重要；同时，逼真的虚假档案可能在选举前操纵公众舆论感知。已有验证多集中于智能体与人类行为的一致性，很少关注智能体是否按其被赋予的档案行事。

Method: 对8名塞尔维亚被试进行问卷、深度访谈和书面自我陈述以建立档案，记录其对68条社交媒体帖子的反应；随后让4种语言模型在5种提示条件下预测这些反应，提示条件在档案内容与指令风格上有所变化。

Result: 态度性内容在预测上大幅优于人口学背景故事；智能体比被试本人更贴近其自述档案；一旦提供档案信息，一致性与保真度无关；"直觉、即时"而非分析性的指令取得所有条件中最高的保真度，并将个体差异压缩从人类水平的7倍降至3倍；该优势在问卷未涉及话题的帖子上依然成立，达到所有设置中的最高保真度，并大幅超过群体基线。

Conclusion: 以直觉方式提示的智能体或可作为通用模拟用户，而非仅限于其被建档话题的专家。结果对语言模型开发具有启示，因为基于直觉的设置在某些任务上比基于推理的设置更适用。

Abstract: Platform policies are increasingly tested on artificial users, making agent fidelity important. Yet convincing fake profiles could also manipulate perceived public opinion before elections. Validation has concentrated on agreement with human behaviour and has paid little attention to whether an agent behaves in line with the profile it was given. The present study profiled eight Serbian participants through a questionnaire, a deep interview, and a written self-presentation, recorded their reactions to sixty-eight social media posts, and asked four language models to predict those reactions under five prompt conditions varying profile content and instruction style. Attitudinal content improved prediction over demographic backstories by a wide margin. Agents matched their stated profiles more closely than participants matched their own survey answers, and consistency proved unrelated to fidelity once profile information was present. Instructing models to respond intuitively and immediately rather than analytically gave the highest fidelity of any condition and cut the compression of individual differences from seven times the human level to three. The advantage held on posts about topics the questionnaire never raised, where that condition reached the highest fidelity of any setup and beat a crowd baseline by a wide margin, which suggests that agents prompted this way could serve as general-purpose simulated users rather than specialists on the topics they were profiled for. Results may bear implications for the development of language models, because intuition-based setups appear better suited to some tasks than reasoning-based ones.

</details>


### [68] [Atelier: Learning Local Self-Supervised Features for CryoEM Volumes via Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30569)
*Phillip Lo,Sudarshan Babu,Dari Kimanius,Aly A. Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Atelier，一个自监督框架，通过分摊式隐式神经表示（INR）拟合来为冷冻电镜重构图提取坐标条件下的连续局部特征，在 8 项体素级属性预测任务上优于仅用体数据的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电镜图谱解析需要空间局部化、跨样本一致且跨空间尺度有信息量的特征。现有深度学习标注方法多从固定体素网格中提取特征，缺乏尺度无关性；而为每张图单独拟合一个 INR 成本过高，且所得表示跨样本不对齐，难以用于大规模特征提取。

Method: 提出 Atelier：一个基于 Transformer 的超网络，对重建冷冻电镜图谱的 INR 拟合进行分摊（amortization）。在 5,439 个 EMDB 图谱上预训练，为多种蛋白质结构（含大型多亚基组装体）生成高保真重建。预训练 Transformer 生成的 INR 通过其中间激活，可在任意空间查询点暴露连续、局部的特征场；这些坐标条件特征作为辅助通道，接入从头训练的 3D 嵌套 U-Net 标注头。

Result: Atelier 可在广泛蛋白质结构上实现高保真重建；其坐标条件特征作为辅助通道后，在 8 项体素级属性预测任务上均优于仅使用体数据的基线。体素网格与 patch-tokenizer 架构无法自然提供此类连续局部特征场。

Conclusion: 分摊式隐式神经表示是冷冻电镜数据几何感知分析的一种有效基础原语（primitive）。

Abstract: CryoEM map interpretation requires features that are spatially localized, consistent across samples, and informative across spatial scales. Most deep learning methods for map annotation extract features from fixed voxel grids. However, implicit neural representations (INRs) are able to model volumetric data as scale-agnostic, coordinate-conditioned functions. INRs are therefore attractive for cryoEM, but fitting a separate INR for each map is too expensive for large-scale feature extraction and produces representations that are not aligned across samples. We introduce Atelier, a self-supervised framework that amortizes INR fitting for reconstructed cryoEM maps. Pretrained on 5,439 Electron Microscopy Data Bank maps, Atelier is a transformer-based hypernetwork that generates high-fidelity reconstructions across a wide range of protein structures, including large multi-subunit assemblies. Beyond reconstruction, the INR generated by the pretrained transformer exposes a continuous, local feature field through its intermediate activations at any spatial query point, a property that voxel grid and patch-tokenizer architectures do not naturally provide. Used as auxiliary channels to a 3D nested U-Net annotation head trained from scratch, these coordinate-conditioned features improve performance on eight voxel-level property prediction tasks over a volume-only baseline. Our results demonstrate that amortized implicit neural representations are an effective primitive for geometry-aware analysis of cryoEM data.

</details>


### [69] [HARDEN: Constrained Evolutionary Search for Harder, Answer-Preserving Evaluation Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30571)
*Aditya Kumaran,Rahul Singhal,Karime Maamari,Amine Mhedhbi,Pradyumna Tambwekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HARDEN，一种约束进化搜索方法，在不改变期望输出的前提下，将现有评测样例改写为更难的变体，平均降低模型准确率 22.7%，最高达 49.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评测多依赖人工整理的基准数据集，其复杂度难以代表真实企业部署场景，导致评测难度不足、区分度有限，无法充分暴露模型在实际应用中的薄弱环节。因此需要一种能够自动生成更困难但依然有效的评测样例的方法。

Method: HARDEN 采用受约束的进化搜索：保持样例的期望输出固定，沿生成的领域特定复杂度轴对输入进行迭代变异，并在搜索过程中施加可行性约束，包括保持任务语义、现实性与执行有效性；同时以单遍基线在相同可行性检查下作为对照比较。

Result: 在 FinQA、PubMedQA、ContractNLI 三个数据集，以及 Qwen3.5 三个规模（35B-A3B、122B-A10B、397B-A17B）上，HARDEN 使任务-模型准确率平均下降 22.7%，相对使用相同可行性检查的单遍基线最多下降 49.9%。

Conclusion: 进化搜索能够有效生成显著更难且仍然合法的评测样例，为贴近企业级部署的模型评测提供了一条可自动化的难度增强路径。

Abstract: Language models are often evaluated on curated benchmarks that underrepresent the complexity of enterprise deployments. We introduce HARDEN, a constrained evolutionary search method to adapt the input of existing evaluation cases into more challenging variants while keeping their expected outputs fixed. HARDEN searches along generated domain-specific complexity axes while enforcing feasibility constraints such as preserving task semantics, realism, and execution validity. Across FinQA, PubMedQA, and ContractNLI and three Qwen3.5 model scales (35B-A3B, 122B-A10B, and 397B-A17B), HARDEN reduces task-model accuracy by 22.7% on average and by up to 49.9% relative to single-pass baselines using the same feasibility checks. These results show that evolutionary search can produce substantially harder valid evaluation cases.

</details>


### [70] [T-RoPE: Time-Aware Rotary Position Embedding for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30576)
*Yang Liu,Noel Loo,Ali Khanafer,Shuying Sun,Akshay Soni,Zhong Wu,Linjun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 T-RoPE（时间感知旋转位置编码）用于序列生成式推荐，将索引旋转替换为基于时间戳的角度，并证明标准 RoPE 具有时间平移不变性、无法区分季节性语境，而 T-RoPE 可打破该不变性；在多数据集与工业场景中全面取得最优效果，线上 A/B 测试亦有正向提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐借鉴大语言模型（LLM）的序列生成范式与 Transformer 设计，包括为文本设计的 RoPE。但在推荐中，交互索引仅记录事件顺序，不含时间流逝、跨尺度行为周期与日历相位信息，直接沿用仅基于索引的 RoPE 会丢失关键时间语义。

Method: T-RoPE：以基于时间戳的角度替代仅索引的旋转，包含可学习时间系数、多尺度频率库、偏移查询对齐（shifted query alignment）与非平稳键旋转（non-stationary key rotation）；从理论上证明标准 RoPE 即便作用于时间戳仍保持时间平移不变、无法区分季节语境，而 T-RoPE 在保持 RoPE 接口的前提下打破该不变性；并给出前向与反向算法，额外开销随序列长度与头维度线性增长。

Result: 在五个公开基准上，T-RoPE 在所有数据集的全部指标上均最优：稀疏的 PixelRec 上 HR@10 较最强基线提升 78%–130%，Amazon Books 上各指标提升 8%–12%；在超过 60 亿交互的工业级电商数据集上，较 HSTU + Time RAB 骨干各指标提升 13%–82%。消融显示最大增益来自多尺度频率（NDCG@50 +56%）与非平稳键（+4%）。Shop 应用线上 A/B 测试中转化率 +0.33%、订单量 +0.63%。

Conclusion: 时间感知的 T-RoPE 在不改变 RoPE 接口的前提下有效注入真实时间语义，兼顾效果与线性复杂度，适用于大规模生成式推荐系统，并已通过离线多基准、工业级数据与线上 A/B 测试验证。

Abstract: Large-scale recommenders increasingly adopt the sequential generative recipe behind large language models, bringing the Transformer into recommendation along with design choices made for text, including Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE). In language models, RoPE encodes token indices for relative position reasoning, but in recommendation, an interaction index records only event order, saying nothing about elapsed time, behavioral cycles across scales, or calendar phase. We revisit this choice and propose T-RoPE, a time-aware RoPE for sequential generative recommendation that replaces index-only rotation with timestamp-based angles, learnable temporal coefficients, multiscale frequency banks, shifted query alignment, and non-stationary key rotation. We prove that standard RoPE, even on timestamps, remains time-translation invariant and cannot distinguish seasonal contexts, and that T-RoPE breaks this invariance while preserving the RoPE interface.
